# Training Example Review & Fix Plan

**Goal:** Each example must teach the Security Analyst Co-Pilot to analyze ONLY the provided artifact (HAR / log / spec / doc) and return actionable, reproducible verification guidance grounded in that artifact alone — no hallucination, no cross-example contamination.

**Rules per folder:**
- Read context.txt as the sole ground truth for that example
- expected_response.md must reference ONLY paths, IDs, hosts, object types, and tenant values that appear in context.txt
- analysis_explanation.md must describe the exact method used for that specific example
- No generic placeholders (/api/v1/resources, RES-XXXX) unless they appear verbatim in context.txt
- Each checkbox must be ticked only after all three files are verified and fixed

**Total folders:** 5065
